          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/231/#laws.0.34.0">1976 c 231 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Equal payments.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may adjust the salary of an employee to provide equal payments through the year and to make use of modern accounting in preparing the payroll. Adjusted salaries must be based on a year of 2088 working hours. Fractions may be dropped or added in order to permit equal payments even if the salary is then slightly changed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Allocations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall set procedures for allocating and encumbering equal salary payments when a payroll period extends beyond the end of the fiscal year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>Subdivision 4 applies to salaries of state officers and employees payable in equal payments throughout the year notwithstanding any other provision in Minnesota Statutes. No provision of any subsequent law relating to the budget, allotment, and encumbrance system or to appropriations for the payment of salaries of state officers and employees shall be construed as inconsistent with this subdivision except as expressly provided in the subsequent act that subdivision 4 does not apply or is superseded, modified, amended, or repealed.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Payroll duties.</span></h2>
          <p>When the department prepares the payroll for an agency, the commissioner assumes the agency head's duties to make authorized or required deductions from, or employer contributions on, the pay of the agency's employees and to prepare and issue the necessary payments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.<span class="headnote">Voluntary deductions.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may require an employee making a voluntary deduction and the recipient of the deduction to provide information on the amount of or a change in the amount of the deduction. The employee making a voluntary deduction must sign and send the deduction instructions to the intended recipient of the deduction. The intended recipient shall forward the original signed instruction and other required information to the employee's payroll preparer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Branch payrolls.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prepare the payroll for the executive branch. Upon request of the Rules Committee of the senate or house of representatives or the supreme court, as appropriate, the commissioner shall prepare the payrolls of the legislative and judicial branches in a similar way.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Certify hours.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may authorize an official to certify the hours worked for payroll purposes in anticipation of the hours actually worked.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>MS 1974 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/273/#laws.0.3.0">1975 c 273 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe procedures to assure payment is made only for hours worked except:</p>
          <p>(1) for leave under a collective bargaining agreement;</p>
          <p>(2) for leave under a plan according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.18">43A.18</a> or the rules of the Department of Management and Budget; or</p>
          <p>(3) to resolve a formal employee grievance permitted by law or collective bargaining agreement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.8a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8a.<span class="headnote">Overpayment.</span></h2>
          <p>The head of an agency shall release to the commissioner money held for an employee when the commissioner certifies to the head that the money is required to correct an overpayment to an employee. An employee's contribution to a retirement fund may not be released until the person otherwise entitled to the employee's retirement account has been notified of the release certification and is eligible to apply for a refund. Released funds are the equivalent of a refund. Funds may not be released if the employee or a survivor is entitled to an immediate or deferred annuity or to a survivor's benefit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Agencies share.</span></h2>
          <p>If a direct appropriation for payroll preparation is made, the commissioner shall bill an agency for its share of payroll costs. The billing shall be done through the indirect cost billing system. Money collected must be deposited in the general fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.17.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Direct deposit.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/177.23">177.23</a>, the commissioner may require direct deposit for all state employees who are being paid by the state payroll system.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.17.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/414/#laws.0.1.0">1957 c 414 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/0/222/#laws.0.1.0">1961 c 222 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/281/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 281 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/803/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 803 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/32/#laws.0.19.0">Ex1971 c 32 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/435/#laws.0.1.0">1973 c 435 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/231/#laws.0.12.0">1976 c 231 s 12</a>-17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/340/#laws.0.1.0">1977 c 340 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/410/#laws.0.6.0">1977 c 410 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/617/#laws.0.47.0">1980 c 617 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/210/#laws.0.49.0">1981 c 210 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.1.9.0">2003 c 112 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/1/#laws.2.35.0">1Sp2003 c 1 art 2 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.42.0">2008 c 204 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.40.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 40</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.18">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.18 ACCOUNTING, PAYROLL FOR COURTS, LEGISLATURE.</h1>
        <p>The judicial and legislative branches are not required to use the state accounting system or a computerized payroll system.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.18.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/720/#laws.0.74.0">1973 c 720 s 74</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.19">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.19 RETIREMENT, SOCIAL SECURITY DEFICIENCIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.19.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>If a direct appropriation for retirement contributions, benefits, or administrative expenses, or for Social Security contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/355.03">355.03</a>, is determined by the chief administrative official of the agency to which or by the officer to whom the appropriation was made to be insufficient to meet the state's obligation under the program for which it is made for the fiscal year for which it is made, the official or the officer shall certify to the finance committee, the appropriations committee, and the commissioner the amount necessary to meet the deficiency. Upon this certification, the commissioner shall transfer the necessary amounts to the appropriate accounts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.19.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount necessary to make the transfer under subdivision 1 is appropriated from the general fund in the state treasury to the agency to which or to the officer to whom the transfer is made.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.19.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/614/#laws.0.57.0">1980 c 614 s 57</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.17.0">1981 c 224 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/86/#laws.1.5.0">2009 c 86 art 1 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.25">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.25 SALE OF SECURITIES BEFORE MATURITY.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall notify the Board of Investment if invested funds are needed for current purposes before maturity of the securities held. The Board of Investment shall then order the needed amount of securities sold or cashed.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.25.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.10.0">1973 c 492 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.26">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.26 ONE DEPOSITORY ACCOUNT FOR EACH TAX.</h1>
        <p>Notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297F.10">297F.10</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/297G.10">297G.10</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.17">298.17</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.282">298.282</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.39">298.39</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.396">298.396</a>, and similar laws to the contrary relating to the depositing, disposition, or apportionment of tax receipts, the commissioner may use one depository account for each tax. To do so, there must be enough information to identify and dispose of or apportion the tax under law. The commissioner shall ask the appropriate officials for the transfers and necessary certifications. The commissioner may issue directives to carry out this section.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.26.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/720/#laws.0.65.0">1973 c 720 s 65</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/674/#laws.0.6.0">1978 c 674 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/509/#laws.0.4.0">1980 c 509 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/305/#laws.12.5.0">1985 c 305 art 12 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/16/#laws.2.26.0">1Sp1985 c 16 art 2 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.9.1.0">1987 c 268 art 9 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/209/#laws.2.4.0">1989 c 209 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/106/#laws.2.1.0">1997 c 106 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/179/#laws.2.1.0">1997 c 179 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DEPOSITS; TRANSFERS; CARRYFORWARDS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.27">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.27 STATE FUNDS; DEPOSIT; CONTROL BY COMMISSIONER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.27.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Commissioner to comply.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall, in the public interest, control the amount and manner of deposit of state funds in depositories by the commissioner. The commissioner shall comply with the controls.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.27.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Daily record.</span></h2>
          <p>Each day the commissioner shall maintain a record on the department's website of all depositories holding noninterest bearing state deposits in excess of $100,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.27.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Competitive bids.</span></h2>
          <p>The depository for a state account must be selected by competitive bid. The commissioner shall invite bids by written notice to designated depositories. The notice must specify the considerations, financial activities, and conditions the commissioner requires for the bid. The account must be awarded to the lowest bidding depository that can, in the opinion of the commissioner, meet the requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.27.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>In exceptional cases, the commissioner may dispense with bidding. The commissioner shall report the circumstances and reasons to the Legislative Audit Commission within five days after opening the account.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.27.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Charges, compensating balances.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may pay a depository a reasonable charge from appropriated money, maintain appropriate compensating balances with the depository, or purchase non-interest-bearing certificates of deposit from the depository for performing depository related services.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.27.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.8.0">1973 c 492 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/403/#laws.0.2.0">1977 c 403 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/271/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 271 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/345/#laws.1.52.0">1991 c 345 art 1 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/99/#laws.0.8.0">1999 c 99 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.3.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 3</a>,50; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/32/#laws.0.6.0">2009 c 32 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.271">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.271 DEPOSITORIES, DESIGNATION.</h1>
        <p>Where any statute of this state requires or permits a bank or trust company to deposit securities with the commissioner of management and budget, the latter, on the request of such depositor, may designate some other bank or trust company as the depository of such securities under such depository agreement as may be prescribed and approved by the depositor, and which will not deprive the commissioner of management and budget of the control thereof and the charges of such depository shall be paid by the depositing bank or trust company. If such depositing bank or trust company is a member of the Federal Reserve System, the Federal Reserve Bank in this state may be the depository designated by the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.271.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/287/#laws.0.1.0">1933 c 287 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.272">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.272 DEPOSITS OF CERTAIN FUNDS OF PUBLIC CORPORATIONS, SECURITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.272.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Depository to give bond.</span></h2>
          <p>If designated treasurer of any public corporation by any statute of this state, the commissioner of management and budget may deposit any public corporation funds in any bank or trust company in this state designated by the commissioner of management and budget unless otherwise provided in the statutes relating to such public corporation. Such deposits shall be deemed deposits of public funds, and said treasurer may require any bank or trust company in which such funds are deposited to give a corporate surety bond for the repayment of such funds or to deposit collateral securities to secure such deposits. Collateral securities so pledged shall consist of bonds and similar securities which are eligible as collateral security for deposits of state funds deposited in depositories designated by the Executive Council of this state. Such bond or collateral shall be in such amount as shall be fixed by the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.272.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Commissioner of management and budget relieved from liability.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of management and budget shall not be liable for the safekeeping of money deposited by the commissioner of management and budget which are secured by a corporate surety bond or a pledge of collateral securities as herein provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.272.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.271">16A.271</a> to apply.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.271">16A.271</a> shall apply to deposits of securities made pursuant to this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.272.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/298/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 298 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.6.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.273">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.273 INDUCEMENTS TO MAKE DEPOSITS.</h1>
        <p>Every person who shall give or promise to the commissioner of management and budget, or to any other person having the custody or control of state funds, any credit, service, or benefit, except as expressly authorized by law, as an inducement or consideration to or for the deposit, loan, or forbearance of state funds, shall be guilty of bribery or attempted bribery, as the case may be.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.273.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/4">RL s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.275">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.275 AGENCY RECEIPTS; DEPOSIT, REPORT, CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.275.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Deposit receipts.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise provided by law, an agency shall deposit receipts totaling $1,000 or more in the state treasury daily. The depositing agency shall send a report to the commissioner on the disposition of receipts since the last report. The commissioner shall credit the deposits received during a month to the proper funds not later than the first day of the next month.</p>
          <p>Notwithstanding the general rule stated above, the commissioner of revenue is not required to make daily deposits if (1) the volume of tax receipts cannot be processed daily with available resources, or (2) receipts cannot be immediately identified for posting to accounts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.275.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exception.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may authorize an agency to deposit receipts totaling $1,000 or more less frequently than daily for those locations where the agency furnishes documentation to the commissioner that the cost of making daily deposits exceeds the lost interest earnings and the risk of loss or theft of the receipts.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.275.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/231/#laws.0.18.0">1976 c 231 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/2/1/#laws.0.4.0">2Sp1981 c 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.103.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 103</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.18.4.0">1987 c 268 art 18 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/275/#laws.0.5.0">1987 c 275 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/392/#laws.1.3.0">2010 c 392 art 1 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.276">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.276 CASH OVERAGE AND SHORTAGE ACCOUNT.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner may keep accounts to record daily the difference between actual and recorded cash receipts including losses from forged and uncollectible checks. At the end of the fiscal year, the commissioner shall clear the accounts by transferring the balances to the general fund and paying the deficits from operating accounts of the agencies charged with the deficit.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.276.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/793/#laws.0.48.0">1978 c 793 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/7/#laws.2.5.0">1997 c 7 art 2 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.28">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.28 TREATMENT OF UNUSED APPROPRIATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Carryforward.</span></h2>
          <p>Agencies may carry forward unexpended and unencumbered nongrant operating balances from the first year of a biennium into the second year of the biennium.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Use of carryforward.</span></h2>
          <p>No money shall be carried forward without the approval of the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Lapse.</span></h2>
          <p>Any portion of any appropriation not carried forward and remaining unexpended and unencumbered at the close of a fiscal year lapses to the fund from which it was originally appropriated. Any appropriation amounts not carried forward and remaining unexpended and unencumbered at the close of a biennium lapse to the fund from which the appropriation was made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reinstatement; final lapse.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may reinstate a lapsed appropriation within three months of the lapse. A reinstated appropriation lapses again no later than three months after it first lapsed. A payment under a reinstated appropriation may be made only under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.15#stat.16A.15.3">16A.15, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Permanent improvements.</span></h2>
          <p>An appropriation to acquire or better public land or buildings or other public improvements of a capital nature, including the acquisition of real property does not lapse until the purposes of the appropriation are determined by the commissioner, after consultation with the affected agencies, to be accomplished or abandoned. This subdivision also applies to any part of an appropriation for a fiscal year that has been requisitioned to acquire real property or construct permanent improvements. An appropriation to pay moving expenses lapses at the end of the third fiscal year during which it was made available.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Canceled October 15.</span></h2>
          <p>On October 15 all allotments and encumbrances for the last fiscal year shall be canceled unless an agency head certifies to the commissioner that there is an encumbrance for services rendered, goods ordered, or grants issued in the last fiscal year, or certifies that funding will be carried forward under subdivision 1. Encumbrances for grants issued by June 30 may be certified for a period of one year beyond the year in which the funds were originally appropriated. Services rendered under grant contracts may occur during the certification period. The commissioner may reinstate the part of the cancellation needed to meet the certified encumbrance or charge the certified encumbrance against the current year's appropriation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise expressly provided by law, subdivisions 1 to 6 apply to every appropriation of a stated sum for a specified purpose or purposes heretofore or hereafter made, but do not, unless expressly provided by law, apply to any fund or balance of a fund derived wholly or partly from special taxes, fees, earnings, fines, federal grants, or other sources that are by law appropriated for special purposes by standing, continuing, or revolving appropriations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Historical society.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided by law, an appropriation made to the Minnesota Historical Society, if not spent during the first year, may be spent during the second year of a biennium. An unexpended balance remaining at the end of a biennium lapses and shall be returned to the fund from which appropriated. An appropriation made to the society for all or part of a biennium may be spent in either year of the biennium.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.28.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Disaster assistance.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of management and budget must transfer the unexpended and unencumbered balance of a general fund disaster assistance appropriation that expires as provided under this section or as otherwise provided by law to the disaster assistance contingency account in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/12.221#stat.12.221.6">12.221, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Expired disaster assistance transferred to the disaster assistance contingency account is appropriated as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/12.221#stat.12.221.6">12.221, subdivision 6</a>, regardless of the specific disaster event or purpose for which the expired disaster assistance was originally appropriated.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner must report each transfer to the chairs of the house of representatives Ways and Means Committee and the senate Finance Committee.</p>
          <p>(d) For the purposes of this subdivision, "disaster assistance appropriation" means an appropriation from the general fund to provide cost-share required for federal disaster assistance or to provide other state disaster assistance under chapter 12A or 12B.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.28.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/3">53-18p</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/431/#laws.2.17.0">1939 c 431 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/399/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 399 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/720/#laws.0.77.0">1973 c 720 s 77</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/231/#laws.0.19.0">1976 c 231 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; art 6 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.62.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/192/#laws.0.64.0">1993 c 192 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/369/#laws.0.39.0">1993 c 369 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/254/#laws.1.48.0">1995 c 254 art 1 s 48</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/463/#laws.0.29.0">1996 c 463 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/374/#laws.7.6.0">2002 c 374 art 7 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/24/#laws.0.2.0">2011 c 24 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/312/#laws.7.10.0">2014 c 312 art 7 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/50/#laws.1.9.0">2019 c 50 art 1 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.281">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.281 APPROPRIATIONS TO LEGISLATURE.</h1>
        <p>Except as provided in this section, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.28">16A.28</a> applies to appropriations made to the legislature, the senate, the house of representatives, or its committees or commissions. An appropriation made to the legislature, the senate, the house of representatives, or a legislative commission or committee other than a standing committee, if not spent during the first year, may be spent during the second year of a biennium. An unexpended balance not carried forward and remaining unexpended and unencumbered at the end of a biennium lapses and shall be returned to the fund from which appropriated. Balances may be carried forward into the next biennium and credited to special accounts to be used only as follows: (1) for nonrecurring expenditures on investments that enhance efficiency or improve effectiveness; (2) to pay expenses associated with sessions, interim activities, public hearings, or other public outreach efforts and related activities; and (3) to pay severance costs of involuntary terminations. The approval of the commissioner of management and budget under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.28#stat.16A.28.2">16A.28, subdivision 2</a>, does not apply to the legislature. An appropriation made to the legislature, the senate, the house of representatives, or a standing committee for all or part of a biennium may be spent in either year of the biennium.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.281.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/793/#laws.0.49.0">1978 c 793 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/192/#laws.0.65.0">1993 c 192 s 65</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/156/#laws.2.18.0">2005 c 156 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.283">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.283 APPROPRIATIONS TO COURTS.</h1>
        <p>If an appropriation for the courts or for an agency in the judicial branch for either fiscal year of a biennium is insufficient, the appropriation for the other fiscal year of the biennium is available for it.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.283.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.104.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 104</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.284">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.284 APPROPRIATIONS TO CONSTITUTIONAL OFFICERS.</h1>
        <p>If an appropriation for a constitutional officer for either fiscal year of a biennium is insufficient, the appropriation for the other fiscal year of the biennium is available for it.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.284.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/404/#laws.0.77.0">1987 c 404 s 77</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.2845">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.2845 APPROPRIATIONS TO OFFICE OF THE GOVERNOR.</h1>
        <p>Except as provided in this section, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.28">16A.28</a> applies to appropriations made to the Office of the Governor. An unexpended balance not carried forward and remaining unexpended and unencumbered at the end of a biennium lapses and shall be returned to the fund from which it was appropriated. Balances may be carried forward into the next biennium and credited to special accounts to be used only as follows: (1) for nonrecurring expenditures on investments that enhance efficiency or improve effectiveness; (2) to pay expenses associated with the work of the office, including public outreach efforts and related activities; and (3) to pay severance costs of involuntary terminations. The approval of the commissioner of management and budget under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.28#stat.16A.28.2">16A.28, subdivision 2</a>, does not apply to the Office of the Governor. An appropriation made to the Office of the Governor may be spent in either year of the biennium.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.2845.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/62/#laws.2.60.0">2023 c 62 art 2 s 60</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.285">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.285 ALLOWED APPROPRIATION TRANSFERS.</h1>
        <p>An agency in the executive, legislative, or judicial branch may transfer state agency operational money between programs within the same fund if: (1) the agency first notifies the commissioner as to the type and intent of the transfer; and (2) the transfer is consistent with legislative intent. If an amount is specified for an item within an activity, that amount must not be transferred or used for any other purpose.</p>
        <p>The commissioner shall report the transfers to the chairs of the senate finance and house of representatives ways and means committees.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.285.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/192/#laws.0.66.0">1993 c 192 s 66</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/226/#laws.1.18.0">1995 c 226 art 1 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.286">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.286 TRANSFER; DISASTER ASSISTANCE CONTINGENCY ACCOUNT.</h1>
        <p>(a) If the balance in the disaster assistance contingency account under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/12.221">12.221</a> at the end of a biennium is less than $50,000,000, the commissioner of management and budget must make transfers according to this section.</p>
        <p>(b) If the final general fund closing balance for a biennium exceeds the closing balance projected for that biennium at the end of the previous regular legislative session by at least $50,000,000, the commissioner of management and budget must transfer the difference between $50,000,000 and the balance in the disaster assistance contingency account at the end of the biennium from the general fund to the disaster assistance contingency account.</p>
        <p>(c) If the final general fund closing balance for a biennium exceeds the closing balance projected for that biennium at the end of the previous legislative session by less than $50,000,000, the commissioner of management and budget must transfer the difference between $50,000,000 and the balance in the disaster assistance contingency account at the end of the biennium from the general fund to the disaster assistance contingency account. The amount transferred under this paragraph shall not exceed the difference between the final closing balance for the previous biennium and the closing balance projected for the general fund at the end of the previous regular legislative session.</p>
        <p>(d) For the purposes of this section, the term "regular legislative session" includes a special legislative session to enact the biennial budget.</p>
        <p>(e) If a transfer is required under this section, the transfer must be completed before October 15 following the end of the previous biennium.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.286.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/123/#laws.1.8.0">2024 c 123 art 1 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.287">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.287 TRANSFER; HOUSING SUPPORT.</h1>
        <p>In fiscal year 2025 and each year thereafter, the commissioner of management and budget must transfer $450,000 from the general fund to the housing support account, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/462A.43">462A.43</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.287.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.70.1.0">2024 c 127 art 70 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.16A.30"><b>16A.30</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/156/#laws.2.52.0">2005 c 156 art 2 s 52</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.16A.35"><b>16A.35</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/192/#laws.0.110.0">1993 c 192 s 110</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.36">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.36 GRANTS FROM AND ADVANCES TO UNITED STATES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.36.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Use of grants.</span></h2>
          <p>Money received by the state from the federal government as federal assistance must be used only for the purpose for which the money is received. If required by the proper federal authorities, interest or income arising from the money received may be credited by the commissioner to the particular account for which the money is received and used only for the purpose of that federal assistance program, or may be repaid to the federal treasury. If not so required, the interest or income shall be credited to the general fund or to another fund authorized to receive the interest or income.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.36.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reciprocal interest policy.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may, if required by the federal government or by agreement with the proper federal authorities, establish an equitable policy providing for the state to pay interest on undisbursed federal money, and providing for the federal government to pay interest to the state on state funds advanced for a federal assistance program. The amount needed to pay the interest is appropriated from the general fund or another fund earning the interest on undisbursed federal money. The interest received from the federal government shall be deposited in the fund that lost interest on state funds advanced for a federal assistance program.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.36.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/3">53-18a</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1937/0/25/#laws.0.1.0">1937 c 25 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.14.0">1955 c 863 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/717/#laws.0.8.0">1973 c 717 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/301/#laws.0.92.0">1983 c 301 s 92</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; art 6 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.63.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/275/#laws.0.6.0">1987 c 275 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">CLAIMS; WARRANTS; TRANSACTIONS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.40">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.40 WARRANTS AND ELECTRONIC FUND TRANSFERS.</h1>
        <p>Money must not be paid out of the state treasury except upon the warrant of the commissioner or an electronic fund transfer approved by the commissioner. Warrants must be drawn on printed blanks that are in numerical order. The commissioner shall enter, in numerical order in a payment register, the number, amount, date, and payee for every payment issued.</p>
        <p>The commissioner may require payees to supply their bank routing information to enable the payments to be made through an electronic fund transfer.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.40.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">67</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/4">RL s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1917/0/480/#laws.0.1.0">1917 c 480 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.3.0">1955 c 863 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.105.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 105</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/254/#laws.1.50.0">1995 c 254 art 1 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/220/#laws.10.29.0">2002 c 220 art 10 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.1.10.0">2003 c 112 art 1 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/1/#laws.2.36.0">1Sp2003 c 1 art 2 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.7.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.401">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.401 VIRTUAL PAYMENTS AUTHORIZED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.401.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Virtual payments.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may establish a program to issue virtual payments from the state treasury. Any rebate to the state generated by the program must be deposited in the general fund unless retained under subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.401.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Rebates.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivision 1, rebates attributable to expenditures in funds established in the state constitution or protected by federal law must be returned to the account from which the expenditure originated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.401.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Rebates retained.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may retain a portion of rebates for the administration of this section. Money retained under this subdivision must be deposited in an account in the special revenue fund and is appropriated to the commissioner for the purposes of this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.401.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/12/#laws.2.5.0">1Sp2021 c 12 art 2 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.41">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.41 CLAIMS AGAINST STATE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.41.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Certified.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 1a, when claims against the state are made for which there is an appropriation available, an official with authority to pay a claim shall approve the claim by certifying that the service was performed, the goods or material furnished, or monthly telephone service is in effect. The claim must be sent to the commissioner accompanied by a transmittal form as prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.41.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Exception to certification.</span></h2>
          <p>When a claim against the state is made by a county, municipality, or other governmental subdivision, under an agreement with the commissioner of transportation, and that agreement provides for payment of the state's contractual obligations before commencing the work, certification that the services have been performed or that the goods or materials have been furnished is not required as a prerequisite to payment of the claim.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.41.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Declaration.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may require a claimant to declare that the claim and its amount are just and correct and that no part of it has been paid. The following form may be used:</p>
          <p>"I declare under the penalties of perjury that this claim is just and correct and that no part of it has been paid.</p>
          <p>Signature of Claimant."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.41.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Declaration same as oath.</span></h2>
          <p>To sign the declaration in subdivision 2 is the same as to sign and swear under oath.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.41.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">68</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1905/0/96/#laws.0.1.0">1905 c 96 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1909/0/120/#laws.0.1.0">1909 c 120 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1917/0/480/#laws.0.2.0">1917 c 480 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.4.0">1955 c 863 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/93/#laws.0.1.0">1957 c 93 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/416/#laws.0.1.0">1984 c 416 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; art 6 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/613/#laws.0.2.0">1988 c 613 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.42">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.42 CLAIMS: FORM, APPROVAL, REGISTER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.42.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Form.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall prescribe the form of a claim.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.42.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Approval.</span></h2>
          <p>If the claim is approved, the commissioner shall issue a payment in the amount of the claim.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.42.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.376.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 376</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.42.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Register.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall enter a payment in the payment register as if it were a cash payment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.42.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Invalid claims.</span></h2>
          <p>If the commissioner determines that a claim is invalid after issuing a warrant, the commissioner may void an unpaid warrant. The commissioner is not liable to any holder who took the void warrant for value.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.42.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">69</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1905/0/96/#laws.0.1.0">1905 c 96 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1909/0/120/#laws.0.2.0">1909 c 120 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1909/0/169/#laws.0.1.0">1909 c 169 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1917/0/480/#laws.0.3.0">1917 c 480 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.5.0">1955 c 863 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.106.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 106</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.8.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 8</a>,9,40</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.43">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.43 WARRANT A RECEIPT.</h1>
        <p>The endorsement by the payee of a warrant is a receipt in full for the claim paid by the warrant.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.43.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">70</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1905/0/96/#laws.0.2.0">1905 c 96 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1909/0/120/#laws.0.3.0">1909 c 120 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1917/0/480/#laws.0.4.0">1917 c 480 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.6.0">1955 c 863 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.44">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.44 COMMISSIONER MAY COMPEL TESTIMONY.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner may subpoena, administer oaths to, and examine under oath, the parties and witnesses to any transaction between the state and a person, partnership, or corporation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.44.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">72</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1917/0/498/#laws.0.2.0">1917 c 498 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.7.0">1955 c 863 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.45">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.45 OUTSTANDING UNPAID WARRANTS, CANCELLATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.45.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Cancel; credit.</span></h2>
          <p>Once each fiscal year the commissioner shall cancel upon the books all outstanding unpaid commissioner's warrants that have been issued and delivered on or before June 30 of the preceding year and credit state amounts subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/345.43">345.43</a> and federal amounts to the appropriate account in the federal fund. These warrants are presumed abandoned under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/345.38">345.38</a> and are subject to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/345.31">345.31</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/345.60">345.60</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.45.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/192/#laws.0.110.0">1993 c 192 s 110</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.45.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/192/#laws.0.110.0">1993 c 192 s 110</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.45.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Locating unpaid warrants.</span></h2>
          <p>A person may not seek or receive from another person, or contract with a person for, a fee or compensation for locating outstanding unpaid commissioner's warrants before the warrants have been reported to the commissioner of commerce under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/345.41">345.41</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.45.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">73</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1923/0/288/#laws.0.1.0">1923 c 288 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.8.0">1955 c 863 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/399/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 399 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; art 6 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.64.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.107.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 107</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/345/#laws.1.53.0">1991 c 345 art 1 s 53</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/513/#laws.4.28.0">1992 c 513 art 4 s 28</a>,29; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/250/#laws.1.51.0">1999 c 250 art 1 s 51</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.46">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.46 LOST OR DESTROYED WARRANT DUPLICATE; INDEMNITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.46.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Duplicate warrant.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner may issue a duplicate of an unpaid warrant to an owner if the owner certifies that the original was lost or destroyed. The commissioner may require certification be documented by affidavit. The commissioner may refuse to issue a duplicate of an unpaid state warrant. If the commissioner acts in good faith, the commissioner is not liable, whether the application is granted or denied.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.46.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Original warrant is void.</span></h2>
          <p>When the duplicate is issued, the original is void. The commissioner may require an indemnity bond from the applicant to the state for double the amount of the warrant for anyone damaged by the issuance of the duplicate. The commissioner is not liable to any holder who took the void original warrant for value, whether or not the commissioner required an indemnity bond from the applicant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.46.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Unpaid refund or rebate.</span></h2>
          <p>For an unpaid refund or rebate issued under a tax law administered by the commissioner of revenue that has been lost or destroyed, an affidavit is not required for the commissioner to issue a duplicate if the duplicate is issued to the same name and Social Security number as the original warrant and that information is verified on a tax return filed by the recipient.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.46.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">74</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/4">RL s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.9.0">1955 c 863 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.13.2.0">2000 c 490 art 13 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.1.11.0">2003 c 112 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.18.1.0">2013 c 143 art 18 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.461">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.461 DUPLICATE BONDS ISSUED.</h1>
        <p>When any bond, certificate of indebtedness, or other written obligation of the state, issued by the state or by any department, bureau, board, or other agency of the state government according to law, has been lost, destroyed, or stolen, a duplicate of such obligation, with unpaid interest coupons, if any, which were attached at the time of the loss, destruction, or theft, shall be issued to the owner, the owner's guardian, or the representative of the owner's estate, as hereinafter provided, upon the furnishing of satisfactory proof of ownership and of such loss, destruction, or theft to the authority empowered to approve indemnity bonds, as hereinafter provided, and upon the certification of the approval of such proof by such authority to the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.461.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/192/#laws.0.1.0">1929 c 192 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.462">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.462 EXECUTION OF DUPLICATES.</h1>
        <p>Such duplicate obligation shall be prepared by the commissioner of management and budget and shall be an exact and complete copy of the original, including the signatures, but need not be a facsimile. Each duplicate obligation shall have written or printed thereon a certificate, the form of which shall be approved by the attorney general, stating, in substance, that the obligation is a duplicate issued pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.461">16A.461</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.464">16A.464</a> with like force and effect as the original. The certificate shall be signed by the commissioner of management and budget, attested by the secretary of state, and sealed with the great seal of the state, and bear the approval of the attorney general as to the issuance of the duplicate and the form of the certificate. Each duplicate shall have plainly written or printed thereon across the face or upon the margin the word "duplicate." Each coupon attached to the duplicate obligation shall have plainly written or printed thereon in like manner the word "duplicate," followed by the date of issue and the signature or facsimile signature of the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.462.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/192/#laws.0.2.0">1929 c 192 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.463">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.463 DELIVERY OF DUPLICATES; BOND.</h1>
        <p>Such duplicate obligation when executed shall be delivered by the commissioner of management and budget to the owner of the original obligation, the owner's guardian, or the representative of the owner's estate; provided, such owner, guardian, or representative shall first file with the commissioner a bond in the full amount of such obligation and unpaid interest to maturity, with sufficient sureties, approved by the same authority as state depository bonds, indemnifying the state against any loss thereon by reason of the existence of the original obligation or any coupon thereto attached, unless such bond is waived as hereinafter provided; and, provided, such owner, guardian, or representative shall furnish satisfactory proof to the commissioner that such original obligation and coupons have not been found or presented for payment up to the time of such delivery; and, if any thereof have been found or presented, duplicates shall be delivered only of such as have not been found or presented. A record of the issuance and delivery of each duplicate obligation and attached coupons shall be made by the commissioner. Such duplicate obligations and coupons, when issued and delivered as hereinbefore provided shall have the same force and effect as the originals.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.463.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/192/#laws.0.3.0">1929 c 192 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.2.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 2</a>,50; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.464">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.464 BOND, WHEN CANCELED.</h1>
        <p>The authority empowered to approve the indemnity bond required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.463">16A.463</a> may waive such bond, in its discretion, at any time six years after the date of the maturity of such lost, destroyed, or stolen bond, certificate of indebtedness, or other written obligation of the state, in any special case where it deems that the person entitled to a duplicate is unable to furnish such indemnity bond without hardship and that it is improbable that the original obligation will ever be found or presented for payment. Such waiver shall be certified to the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.464.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/192/#laws.0.4.0">1929 c 192 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.50.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 50</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.47">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.47 COMMISSIONER'S ACCOUNT, DOCUMENT DUTIES.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner shall make and keep in the department's office a record of all accounts and documents required by law to be returned to or filed with the commissioner. The commissioner shall file and keep all official receipts and vouchers. The commissioner shall also keep an account for each appropriation, showing the disbursements. The commissioner shall keep other accounts needed to show the daily condition of state finances.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.47.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">75</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/4">RL s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.10.0">1955 c 863 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/13/#laws.0.108.0">1Sp1985 c 13 s 108</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.48">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.48 REFUND OF ERRONEOUS DEPOSITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.48.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>A verified claim may be submitted to the concerned agency head for refund of money in the treasury to which the state is not entitled. The claimant must submit with the claim a complete statement of facts and reasons for the refund. The agency head shall consider and approve or disapprove the claim, attach a statement of reasons, and forward the claim to the commissioner for settlement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.48.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount needed to pay a refund under subdivision 1 is appropriated to the person entitled to it from the fund to which the money was credited.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.48.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1947/0/416/#laws.0.1.0">1947 c 416 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.11.0">1955 c 863 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.19.1.0">1987 c 268 art 19 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/513/#laws.4.30.0">1992 c 513 art 4 s 30</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.49">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.49 REFUNDS OF $1 OR LESS.</h1>
        <p>A refund of $1 or less may not be paid from the treasury unless the receipts giving rise to the refund were $1 or less. The commissioner shall set requirements for the small refunds, which may differ from the procedure in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16A.48">16A.48</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.49.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/48/#laws.0.69.0">Ex1967 c 48 s 69</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.4.0">1973 c 492 s 4</a> subd 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">REPORTS; FUNDS; ACCOUNTS; PROJECTS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.50">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.50 FINANCIAL REPORT TO LEGISLATURE.</h1>
        <p>By December 31 of each year, the commissioner shall report to the legislature on the operation of all state funds during the last fiscal year. The report shall contain financial statements and disclosures which show the state's financial operations and position. The report must conform with generally accepted government accounting principles.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.50.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/4">79</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/4">RL s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/847/#laws.0.1.0">1955 c 847 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/863/#laws.0.12.0">1955 c 863 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/51/#laws.0.1.0">1959 c 51 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/406/#laws.0.56.0">1974 c 406 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/314/#laws.0.2.0">1979 c 314 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/301/#laws.0.93.0">1983 c 301 s 93</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.501">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.501 REPORT ON EXPENDITURE OF BOND PROCEEDS.</h1>
        <p>(a) The commissioner of management and budget must report annually to the legislature on the degree to which entities receiving appropriations for capital projects in previous omnibus capital improvement acts have encumbered or expended that money. The report must be submitted to the chairs of the house of representatives Ways and Means Committee and the senate Finance Committee by January 1 of each year.</p>
        <p>(b) The commissioner of management and budget must report by January 15 of each year to the chairs and ranking minority members of the house of representatives and senate committees with jurisdiction over capital investment, finance, and ways and means on the amount and percentage of each agency's capital appropriation that is used to pay for the costs of staff directly attributable to capital programs or projects funded with state general obligation bond proceeds. The report must also include information on agencies' compliance with the commissioner's policies governing the use of general obligation bond proceeds to pay staff costs and any changes to the commissioner's policies.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.501.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/643/#laws.0.33.0">1994 c 643 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/404/#laws.0.31.0">1998 c 404 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/393/#laws.0.33.0">2002 c 393 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.1.12.0">2003 c 112 art 1 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/1/#laws.2.37.0">1Sp2003 c 1 art 2 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/189/#laws.0.28.0">2010 c 189 s 28</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.502">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.502 NONSTATE COMMITMENTS TO CAPITAL PROJECTS.</h1>
        <p>If a state appropriation or grant for a capital project or project phase is not sufficient, by itself, to complete the project or project phase, and thus requires a commitment from other sources:</p>
        <p>(1) the commitment, including any required match, must be in an amount that, when added to the appropriation or grant, is sufficient to complete the project or project phase; and</p>
        <p>(2) the appropriation or grant is not available until the commissioner has determined that the commitment is sufficient.</p>
        <p>In making the determination, the commissioner must apply generally accepted governmental accounting standards and principles, including those that are particularly applicable to capital projects.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.502.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/20/#laws.1.29.0">2005 c 20 art 1 s 29</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.16A.51"><b>16A.51</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/654/#laws.2.155.0">1984 c 654 art 2 s 155</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.16A.52"><b>16A.52</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.4.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 4</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.53">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.53 BOOKKEEPING ACCOUNTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.53.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Funds and accounts created by law.</span></h2>
          <p>When a law creates a fund or account in the treasury into which are deposited certain revenues and out of which certain expenditures are appropriated, the commissioner may consider the creation of the fund or account as the creation of a bookkeeping account in the state's accounting system so as to reflect the revenues deposited in the treasury and credited to the bookkeeping account and the expenditures appropriated from the treasury and charged to the bookkeeping account. The commissioner must organize these bookkeeping accounts into funds in accordance with generally accepted accounting principles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.53.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exception.</span></h2>
          <p>Subdivision 1 does not apply to a fund created by the constitution or to a fund required to be created in the treasury by federal law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.53.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Commissioner to manage funds and accounts.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) As necessary, the commissioner may eliminate an account that is no longer needed for the purposes specified for it in law.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner must eliminate an account that meets the criteria in paragraph (c) unless the commissioner determines that the account is necessary for efficient fiscal operation.</p>
          <p>(c) Criteria for account elimination are:</p>
          <p>(1) receipts to the account and transfers into the account average less than $1,000 per year in the past four years;</p>
          <p>(2) year-end balances in the past four years average less than $1,000 per year; and</p>
          <p>(3) the account has been in existence for at least four years.</p>
          <p>(d) Any balances in an eliminated account must be transferred to the general fund unless some other disposition is specified in law. If the commissioner eliminates an account established in law, the commissioner must notify the legislature, in a report to the appropriate finance committees, of the elimination.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.53.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Report.</span></h2>
          <p>Each agency that manages accounts within a fund must report at least annually to the appropriate finance committees of the legislature on the number, purpose, and recent financial activity in those accounts. The commissioner must establish uniform criteria and timing for the reports.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.16A.53.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/30/#laws.0.2.0">1959 c 30 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/628/#laws.2.1.0">1984 c 628 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/284/#laws.1.1.0">2004 c 284 art 1 s 1</a>-3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.16A.531">
        <h1 class="shn">16A.531 FUNDS CREATED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.531.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Environmental fund.</span></h2>
          <p>There is created in the state treasury an environmental fund as a special revenue fund for deposit of receipts from environmentally related taxes, fees, and other sources as provided in subdivision 1a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.16A.531.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Revenues.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The following revenues must be deposited in the environmental fund:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) revenue from the motor vehicle transfer fee as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115A.908">115A.908</a>, subdivision 2;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) all fees collected under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116.07#stat.116.07.4d">116.07, subdivision 4d</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) all money collected by the Pollution Control Agency in enforcement matters as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/115.073">115.073</a>;</p>
